# 序

习近平主席在致2024世界中文大会的贺信中指出，语言是人类交流思想的工具、传承文明的载体、增进理解的桥梁。中文承载着中华民族数千年的文明智慧，是中国贡献给世界的重要公共文化产品，支持服务国际社会开展好中文教育是中国作为母语国的责任。

作为中国教育部推出的服务全球中文学习者的标准化语言测量工具，中文水平考试HSK历经三十余年发展，已成为全球最具影响力和权威性的中文水平测评体系。截至目前，HSK已在全球168个国家设立1477个考点、3万余间考场，累计服务考生820万人。HSK不仅成为评估国际中文教育质量的重要标尺，更在吸引国际学生来华留学、助力海外人才在中资及跨国企业就业实习等方面发挥着日益重要的作用。

当前，新一轮科技革命与产业变革加速演进，人工智能等新技术正在重塑教育形态，对人才需求产生深远影响。面对海外中文学习需求的爆发式增长，国际中文教育迎来新的发展机遇，也对HSK的服务能力与测评体系提出更高要求。为此，我们积极推进HSK向3.0版本升级，将原有一至六级考试体系调整为覆盖初、中、高级各水平人群的三等九级新体系，并根据社会经济生活发展，调整HSK话题和词汇，增加中国文化、中国当代发展等考查内容，帮助全球学习者了解真实、立体、全面的中国；加强“中文+学术”“中文+专业”“中文+职业”等内容，帮助国际人才精准掌握中文、拓宽专业领域；推动人工智能全面赋能测评体系，加强AI命题、AI评分及AI自适应学习产品开发，主动适应并引领智能时代的教育变革。

以HSK3.0为依托，汉考国际（CTI）联合外语教学与研究出版社，汇聚专家智慧，开发了《新HSK教程》。本教程坚持以学习者为中心，支持课堂教学、自主学习和AI助学等多种方式，旨在全面提升学习者的中文交际能力、认知水平与思辨素养。很高兴能向各国中文学习者推荐《新HSK教程》，期待这套教程能够助力更多国际学子掌握中文、走向世界，为推动构建人类命运共同体贡献语言之力！

最后，谨向所有参与HSK发展与教程编写的专家同仁、全球合作伙伴致以诚挚谢意！

郁云峰

中国教育部中外语言交流合作中心主任

2025年12月

# 前言

作为新一代多功能教材，《新HSK教程》系列（以下简称“新教程”）的设计、编写继续秉持HSK“以学习者为中心”的理念，并综合借鉴当代语言学、第二语言教学等理论成果，进一步拓展实践空间。“以学习者为中心”的理念强调新教程不是单一的HSK考试用书，而是将HSK考试与中文教学、学习有机融合，通过课堂教学、自主学习、AI助学的方式进一步提升学习者的中文交际能力、认知水平与思辨素养，帮助其成为精通中文、融通中外的优秀人才。

新教程1～6级分为主干课本和学练手册两种，主干课本聚焦课堂教学与核心内容传授，学练手册则是对主干课本内容的延伸和补充。学练手册练习题深度对标HSK3.0考试，学习者在练习的过程中既能了解自身中文掌握情况，也能熟悉HSK3.0考试题型，从而实现从容备考。此外，新教程的主干课本在编写中突出交际情景化、结构简约化，同时借助数字手段使教材资源呈现立体化特征。

由于中文对交际语境高度依赖，情景的重要性不言而喻。为此，新教程借鉴情景剧形式设计交际场景及交际任务，以一系列独立的情景剧单元组织内容。例如，新教程设计了海外和中国国内两类场景，故事线索围绕数位主要角色展开，不仅演绎中文学习者的校园生活、个人成长点滴，也展现中国人的日常生活、学习工作图景，讲述中外交往、友谊生辉的故事，让学习者在真实的场景和故事中学习中文，了解真实、立体、全面的中国。

为满足AI时代的泛在化学习需求，新教程采取教案化、学案化设计流程，更方便教学或自学。新教程编写期间，恰逢AI技术蓬勃发展，数字技术的全方位赋能使得教材资源立体化建设成为可能。新教程设计了一位AI助教——小语，她是将纸版教材和数字资源连接起来的灵魂性人物，学习者找到小语，即可获得相关资源。例如，学习者可以通过“小语助力”“小语讲堂”等板块进行语言知识及语言技能的学习，通过敲“小语的彩蛋”获取更多数字资源，既有趣又方便。未来我们还将持续开发线上课程，让小语成为您教中文、学中文的好助手。

新教程策划及编写过程中，得到了中外语言交流合作中心、汉考国际、外语教学与研究出版社以及业内许多专家学者的鼎力支持与专业指导。现在，这套凝聚着集体智慧的教材终于和大家见面了，我们诚挚欢迎海内外使用者及时反馈使用中遇到的问题，帮助新教程不断完善。感谢您的支持！

编者

2025年12月

### 使用说明

《新HSK教程1 学练手册》（以下简称“学练手册”）是《新HSK教程1》（以下简称“主干课本”）的配套用书，是主干课本内容的补充、延伸与辅助。

本级学练手册包含“学、练、测”三部分：“学”是与主干课本相关的可进一步学习的内容，“练”是匹配学习内容及HSK考试题型的强化练习，“测”则是围绕HSK考试进行的测查练习。

据此，我们设计了“小语讲堂”“模拟测练”和“模拟测试”三大板块。

“小语讲堂”补充语音与汉字知识，夯实语音和书写基础。语音内容在第1～15课，汉字内容在第7～15课，教师与学习者可按需选学选练。

“模拟测练”从第4课起，将HSK考试题型与课后检测结合，测练一体，题型多样，题量适中，可用于检验每课学习效果。

“模拟测试”是一套完整的HSK（一级）模拟考卷，用于在学完一级全部内容后进行综合测评。

建议师生在教学或学习中配合主干课本使用本手册，科学合理地统筹教学及学习进度。

本书还配有数字教材，可通过“外研国际智慧教育平台”（https://global.unipus.cn）获取。数字教材涵盖本书配套的音频、视频、交互式学习内容、补充练习等丰富资源。数字教材激活说明请扫描封底二维码查看。本书配套资源可通过“外研社汉语教学资源网”（http://www.fltrp-clt.com）查看并下载。

# INSTRUCTIONS FOR USE

The New HSK Course 1 Workbook (hereinafter referred to as the “Workbook”) is a companion book for the New HSK Course 1 (hereinafter referred to as the “Textbook”), serving to supplement, extend, and support the content of the Textbook.

This Workbook comprises three components: “Study”, “Practice”, and “Test”. The “Study” component provides supplementary learning materials related to the Textbook; the “Practice” component offers intensive exercises aligned with the learning content and the HSK exam formats; and the “Test” component features assessment exercises centered around the HSK exam.

Based on this framework, we have designed three major sections: “Xiaoyu’s Classroom”, “Simulated Practice”, and “Mock Test”.

The “Xiaoyu’s Classroom” section supplements knowledge of pronunciation and Chinese characters, solidifying the foundation in speaking and writing. Pronunciation content spans Lessons 1–15, while Chinese character content covers Lessons 7–15. Teachers and learners may use these materials selectively as needed.

The “Simulated Practice” section integrates HSK question types with post-lesson assessments starting from Lesson 4. Combining practice with assessment, it presents diverse question types and a moderate volume of exercises to evaluate the learning outcomes of each lesson.

The “Mock Test” section provides a complete HSK Level 1 practice exam upon the completion of all Level 1 content, intended for comprehensive assessment.

Teachers and learners are advised to use this Workbook alongside the Textbook to systematically coordinate teaching and learning progress.

The Workbook comes with a digital edition, accessible via the FLTRP Global Smart Education Platform (https://global.unipus.cn). This platform offers audio, video, interactive modules, and extra exercises. To activate your digital edition, please scan the QR code located on the back cover. Additional teaching and learning resources can be viewed and downloaded from FLTRP CLT Resources (http://www.fltrp-clt.com).

### 2 声母和韵母（1） Initials and Finals (1)

汉语声母和韵母拼合组成音节，但是声母和韵母不是都能相拼的。

在下方表格列举的部分声母中，除了声母“n、l”，其他声母都不能和韵母“u”及“u”开头的韵母相拼。另外，声母“b、p、f”不和韵母“e”相拼；声母“f、g、k、h”不和韵母“i”相拼。

韵母 “er” 不和任何声母相拼，而是单独作为一个音节。

In Chinese, initials and finals combine to form syllables, but not all initials can be paired with all finals.

Among the initials listed in the table below, except for the initials “n” and “l”, no other initials can combine with the final “U” or the finals beginning with “U”. In addition, the initials “b”, “p”, and “f” do not combine with the final “e”; the initials “f”, “g”, “k”, and “h” do not combine with the final “i”.

The final “er” does not combine with any initial and stands alone as an independent syllable.

##### 先跟读声母和韵母，再大声朗读。

Listen and repeat the initials and finals, then read aloud.

### 3 声调 Tones

汉语有4个基本声调，包括第一声（55）、第二声（35）、第三声（214）和第四声（51），也称一声、二声、三声、四声。声调要标在韵母的主要元音上。声调不同，意义也不同。

There are four basic tones in Chinese: the first tone (55), the second tone (35), the third tone (214), and the fourth tone (51). Tone marks are placed above the main vowel of the final. Different tones can change the meaning of a syllable.

### 小语讲堂

Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 一、语音

Pronunciation

☐)2-1

##### ① 声母和韵母（2） Initials and Finals (2)

声母 “j、q、x” 只和下方表格中的韵母 “i [i]、u” 及 “i [i]、u” 开头的韵母相拼，不和韵母 “a、u” 及 “a、u” 开头的韵母直接相拼。

The initials “j”, “q”, and “x” combine only with the finals “i [i]” and “u”, and with the finals beginning with “i [i]” and “u”. They do not combine with the finals “a” or “u”, or with the finals beginning with “a” or “u”.

### 2 拼写规则 Spelling Rules

（1）韵母 “i、u、u” 单独成音节时的写法 Writing the finals “i”，“u”，and “u” as independent syllables

韵母 “i、u、u” 单独成音节时，写成 “yi、wu、yu”。

When the finals “i”, “u”, and “u” stand alone as syllables, they are written as “yi”, “wu”, and “yu” respectively.

##### 先跟读音节，再大声朗读。

Listen and repeat the syllables, then read aloud.



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yī</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yí</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yǐ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yì</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wū</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wú</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wǔ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wù</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yū</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yú</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yǔ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yù</td></tr></table>

yǔ雨

wǔ

五

1

yī

yǐzi

椅子

（2）声母“j、q、x”和韵母“u”相拼的规则 Rule for combining the initials “j”，“q”，and“x”with the final“u”

声母“j、q、x”和韵母“u”相拼时，“u”上边的两点儿省略，写作“u”。

When the initials “j”，“q”，and“x”combine with the final“u”，the two dots of “u”are omitted，and it is written as“u”.

##### 先跟读音节，再大声朗读。

Listen and repeat the syllables, then read aloud.



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>jū</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>jú</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>jǔ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>jù</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>qū</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>qú</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>qǔ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>qù</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>xū</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>xú</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>xǔ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>xù</td></tr></table>

（3）韵母“iou”单独成音节时的写法 Writing the final “iou” as an independent syllable 韵母“iou”单独成音节时，写作“you”。 When the final “iou” stands alone as a syllable, it is written as “you”.

先跟读音节，再大声朗读。

Listen and repeat the syllables, then read aloud.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//b8c0423b-dfcb-4fa1-bb8b-5840704ee573/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_237_797_588_857.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-14T10%3A47%3A32Z%2F-1%2F%2Fc105f646d43b2ce0638f34f01b4befbb920f754185eb1479868f996289abbbad" alt="Image" width="29%" /></div>


yóuyǒng lǚyóu péngyou

（4）韵母 “iou” 在音节里的写法 Writing the final “iou” in syllables

韵母 “iou” 和声母 “m、d、n、l、j、q、x” 相拼时，写作 “iu”。例如：“miu、diu、niu、liu、jiu、qiu、xiu”。“iu” 的声调要标在 “u” 上。

When the final “iou” combines with the initials “m”, “d”, “n”, “l”, “j”, “q”, and “x”, it is written as “iu”. For example: “miu”, “diu”, “niu”, “liu”, “jiu”, “qiu”, and “xiu”. The tone mark for the “iu” is placed above the “u”.

先跟读音节，再大声朗读。

Listen and repeat the syllables, then read aloud.

miù diū niú liǔ jiù qiū qiú xiǔ

## 一、语音

Pronunciation ◀) 3-1

#### ① 声母和韵母（3） Initials and Finals (3)

声母 “zh、ch、sh、r、z、c、s” 主要和下方表格中列举的部分韵母相拼，不和韵母 “i [i]、u” 及 “i [i]、u” 开头的韵母相拼。

The initials “zh”, “ch”, “sh”, “r”, “z”, “c”, and “s” primarily combine with some of the finals shown in the table below, but they do not combine with the finals “i [i]” or “U”, or with the finals beginning with “i [i]” or “U”.

##### 先跟读声母和韵母，再大声朗读。

Listen and repeat the initials and finals, then read aloud.

### 2 拼写规则 Spelling Rules

（1）“u+韵母”类单独成音节时的写法 Writing the finals beginning with “u” as independent syllables

韵母 “ua、uo、uai、uei、uan、uen、uang、ueng” 单独成音节时，“u” 写作 “w”，写成 “wa、wo、wai、wei、wan、wen、wang、weng”。

For the finals beginning with “u”, replace the initial “u” with “w” to form independent syllables. Thus, “ua” changes to “wa”, “uo” to “wo”, “uai” to “wai”, “uei” to “wei”, “uan” to “wan”, “uen” to “wen”, “uang” to “wang”, and “ueng” to “weng”.

##### 先跟读音节，再大声朗读。

Listen and repeat the syllables, then read aloud.



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wā</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wá</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wǎ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wà</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wēi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wéi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wěi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>wèi</td></tr></table>

（2）韵母“uei、uen”在音节里的写法 Writing the finals“uei”and“uen”in syllables 韵母“uei、uen”和声母相拼时，写作“ui、un”。例如：“shuí、chǔn、suì、cūn”。When the finals“uei”and“uen”combine with an initial，they are written as“ui”and“un”. For example:“shuí”，“chǔn”，“suì”，and“cūn”.

1 两个三声音节的连读变调 Tone Sandhi in Two Consecutive Third-Tone Syllables 两个三声（√）音节连读时，第一个三声读成二声，即214+214→35+214。

When two third-tone ( $ ^{\circ} $) syllables occur in succession, the first is pronounced as a second tone, i.e., 214+214 $ \rightarrow $35+214.

大声拼读。注意三声变调。

Spell and read aloud. Note the sandhi of the third tone.

### 2 轻声 Neutral Tone

除四个基本声调外，汉语中有些音节不带声调，读音又轻又短，被称为轻声。轻声通常不做声调标记。例如：“jiǎozi、bàba”。

In addition to the four basic tones, some syllables in Chinese are pronounced without a specific tone and sound light and short; these are referred to as the neutral tone. In writing, the neutral tone is usually unmarked. For example: “jiǎozi” and “bàba”.

汉语中一些词语读不读轻声，意义会不一样。例如：

In Chinese, whether certain words are pronounced with a neutral tone can change their meanings. For example:

东西 dōngxī 方位，东边和西边 directions, east and west

买卖 mǎi mài 买和卖 buying and selling

孙子 Sūnzi ☐ 人名，中国古代军事家 an ancient Chinese military strategist

东西 dōngxi 事物 objects

买卖 mãimai 生意 business

孙子 sūnzi 儿子的儿子 grandson

### 1 听录音，选择听到的音节并大声朗读。

Listen and repeat the syllables, then read aloud. Note the sandhi of the third tone.

### 小语讲堂

Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 一、语音

Pronunciation

☐) 5-1

### 1 “不”的变调 Tone Sandhi of“不”

“不（bù）”单用或在一声、二声和三声音节前，不变调。在四声音节前读二声。

The character “不 (bù)” retains its original tone when used independently or placed before a first-, second-, or third-tone syllable. However, when it precedes a fourth-tone syllable, it is pronounced as a second tone.

 $$  不 \left(b\text{ù}\right)+ 四声 \rightarrow 不 \left(b\text{ú}\right)+ 四声 $$ 

不(bù) + fourth tone → 不(bú) + fourth tone

先跟读音节，再大声朗读。注意“不”的变调。

Listen and repeat the syllables, then read aloud. Note the tone sandhi of “不”.

bù（不）+shì（是） → bú+shì（不是）

bù（不）+huì（会） → bú+huì（不会）

bù（不）+dà（大） → bú+dà（不大）

bù（不）+duì（对） → bú+duì（不对）

### 2 “—”的变调 Tone Sandhi of “—”

“—（yī）”单用或表示序数时，不变调。有两种情况需变调。

The character “— (yī)” retains its original tone when used independently or as an ordinal number. However, its tone changes in two specific situations.

（1）“一”在一声、二声和三声音节前，读四声。

When the character “—” precedes a first-, second-, or third-tone syllable, it is pronounced as a fourth tone.

 $$ \begin{aligned}& 一 \left(y\text{ī}\right)+ 一声 / 二声 / 三声 \rightarrow 一 \left(y\text{ì}\right)+ 一声 / 二声 / 三声 \\& 一 \left(y\text{ī}\right)+first/second/third~tone\rightarrow 一 \left(y\text{ì}\right)+first/second/third~tone\end{aligned} $$ 

先跟读音节，再大声朗读。注意“一”的变调。

Listen and repeat the syllables, then read aloud. Note the tone sandhi of “—”.

 $$  y\text{ī}( 一 )+x i\text{ē }( 些 )\quad\rightarrow\quad y i+x i\text{ē }( 一些 ) $$ 

 $$  y\text{ī}( 一 )+nián( 年 )\quad\rightarrow\quad y\text{ì}+nián( 一年 ) $$ 

 $$ y\text{ī (一)})+k\text{ǒu (口)}\quad\rightarrow\quad y\text{ì }+k\text{ǒu (一口)} $$ 

（2）“一”在四声音节前，读二声。

When the character “—” precedes a fourth-tone syllable, it is pronounced as a second tone.

— (yī) + 四声 → — (yí) + 四声

— (yī) + fourth tone → — (yí) + fourth tone

先跟读音节，再大声朗读。注意“一”的变调。

Listen and repeat the syllables, then read aloud. Note the tone sandhi of “—”.

 $$ \begin{aligned}&y\text{ī}( 一 )+g\text{è}( 个 )\quad&\rightarrow\quad&y\text{í}+g\text{è}( 一个 )\\&y\text{ī}( 一 )+r\text{ì}( 日 )\quad&\rightarrow\quad&y\text{í}+r\text{ì}( 一日 )\\&y\text{ī}( 一 )+su\text{ì}( 岁 )\quad&\rightarrow\quad&y\text{í}+su\text{ì}( 一岁 )\end{aligned} $$

### 小语讲堂

Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 一、语音

Pronunciation

☐)6-1

### 1 儿化 Retroflex Final

汉语中一些词后边加上词尾“儿”，表示“细小、喜爱”的感情色彩，有时会改变词义、词性。有的词语直接加“儿”，相应地在原拼音后加“r”，例如：“这儿zhèr、那儿nèr、面条儿miàntiáor”。有的词语后边不直接加“儿”，但口语里习惯上会儿化，例如：“茶馆cháguǎnr、饭馆fànguǎnr、名牌míngpáir、照片zhàopiānr”。

In Chinese, adding “儿 (ér)” to the end of some words can convey connotations of smallness or fondness, and can sometimes change the word’s meaning or part of speech.

In some cases, “儿” is added directly to the end of a word, and accordingly, “r” is added after the original pinyin. For example: “这儿 zhè (here)”, “那儿 nà (there)”, and “面条儿 miàntiáo (noodles)”.

For other words, “儿” is not explicitly added, but they are conventionally pronounced with “儿”. For example: “茶馆 cháguǎnr (teahouse)”, “饭馆 fànguǎnr (restaurant)”, “名牌 míngpáir (famous brand)”, and “照片 zhàopiānr (photo)”.

##### 先跟读，再大声朗读。

Listen and repeat, then read aloud.

### 2 拼写规则——隔音符号 Spelling Rule—The Syllable-Dividing Mark

“a、o、e”开头的音节与前面的音节相接时，为了避免音节界限不清楚，要在两个音节间使用隔音符号（'）进行区分。例如：“shí'èr（十二）、Xī'ān（西安）、Tiān'ān Mén（天安门）”。

When a syllable beginning with “ɑ”, “o”, or “e” follows another syllable, the syllable-dividing mark (') should be used between the two syllables to avoid ambiguity. For example: “shí'èr (十二)”, “Xī'ān (西安)”, and “Tiān'ān Mén (天安门)”.

Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 一、语音

Pronunciation

☐)7-1

① 送气声母和不送气声母辨析 Distinction Between Aspirated and Unaspirated Initials

送气声母和不送气声母是汉语语音的一大特色，送气声母和不送气声母都是成对的，共六组：“b和p、d和t、g和k、j和q、zh和ch、z和c”。

A major feature of Chinese pronunciation is the distinction between aspirated and unaspirated initials. These two types of initials occur in pairs, forming a total of six groups: “b and p”, “d and t”, “g and k”, “j and q”, “zh and ch”, as well as “z and c”.

听录音，分辨送气声母和不送气声母，并大声朗读。

Listen, distinguish between aspirated and unaspirated initials, then read aloud.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//634dfdbf-c0b6-40c8-acdc-5ab13ddc71e3/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_225_989_1034_1192.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-14T10%3A47%3A32Z%2F-1%2F%2F2d5bf6ecd688635477f30f3e077834fb95f2d55be374a7faafaa7914cb9689fd" alt="Image" width="67%" /></div>


2 前鼻音韵母和后鼻音韵母辨析 Distinction Between Front Nasal and Back Nasal Finals

汉语中前鼻音韵母是指以“-n”结尾的韵母，后鼻音韵母是指以“-ng”结尾的韵母。

In Chinese, front nasal finals refer to the finals ending in “-n”, while back nasal finals refer to those ending in “-ng”.

##### 听录音，分辨前鼻音韵母和后鼻音韵母，并大声朗读。

Listen, distinguish between front nasal finals and back nasal finals, then read aloud.

## 二、汉字 Chinese Characters

##### 汉字简介 Introduction to Chinese Characters

汉字发祥于古老的中国，是世界上最古老的文字之一，也是世界上唯一一个从古代演变至今、从未间断使用的文字。

汉字是记录汉语的符号。历史上，汉字经历了甲骨文、金文、篆书、隶书、楷书、草书、行书等形式，这些形式至今仍在书法艺术中被使用。其中，楷书是汉字的标准字体。

汉字以象形字为基础，以形声字为主体，现代汉字中形声字比例高达90%左右，为人们学习汉字提供了便利。

汉字是形、音、义一体的文字形式。一般情况下，根据文字形式，就可推知其意义和读音。例如：“材”，“才”提示该字的读音，“杕”（木字旁）是偏旁，表明与树木相关；“花”，“化”提示该字的读音，“艹”（草字头）是偏旁，表明与花草植物相关。

随着数字时代的到来，借助汉语拼音来识读汉字的途径和方式更加丰富，进一步方便了学习者掌握汉字，提升汉语阅读水平。

Chinese characters originated in ancient China and are among the world's oldest writing systems. They are also the only writing system that has continuously evolved and been used from ancient times to the present day.

Chinese characters serve as the written symbols of the Chinese language. Throughout history, Chinese characters have developed through various scripts, including oracle-bone inscriptions, bronze script, seal script, clerical script, regular script, cursive script, and running

script—which continue to be used in the art of calligraphy today. Among them, the regular script has become the standard form of Chinese writing.

Chinese characters are based on pictographs and are predominantly phono-semantic compound characters, with about 90% belonging to this type. This system makes learning characters more accessible.

A Chinese character integrates form, pronunciation, and meaning. Generally, the form of a character reveals clues about its pronunciation and meaning. For example: in “材”, the component “才” indicates the pronunciation, and the radical “杮” (mùzìpáng) suggests a relation to wood; in “花”, “化” indicates the pronunciation, and the radical “艹”(cǎozitóu) implies a relation to plants and flowers.

With the advent of the digital age, the pathways and methods for recognizing and reading Chinese characters through pinyin have become more diverse, further facilitating learners' mastery of Chinese characters and enhancing their Chinese reading proficiency.

## 二、汉字 Chinese Characters

##### 汉字书写（一） Chinese Character Writing (I)

### 1 汉字结构简介 Introduction to the Structure of Chinese Characters

汉字是记录汉语的符号，一个汉字基本由形、音、义构成，所以，汉字也被称为表意文字或意音文字。

汉字结构主要包括笔画、部件和整字。一般来说，笔画构成部件，部件构成整字。

笔画是构成汉字字形特定的点和线，是汉字的最小结构单位。汉字的常用笔画有横、竖、撇、捺、点、提、折、钩。

部件由笔画组成，有的部件能够独立成字，有的不能成字，需与其他部件组成一个整字。

整字由部件组成。整字结构有两种：一种叫独体字，由一个部件组成；另一种叫合体字，由两个或两个以上的部件组成。

汉字笔画是有书写顺序的，称为笔顺。

Chinese characters are the written symbols used to record the Chinese language. Each character is composed of form, pronunciation, and meaning, which is why Chinese characters are often described as semantic script or semanto phonetic script.

The structure of a Chinese character generally includes strokes, components, and the complete character. Generally, strokes form components, and components form complete characters.

Strokes are specific dots and lines that make up the shape of a character. They are the smallest structural units of Chinese writing. The commonly used strokes include horizontal (横), vertical (竖), left-falling (撇), right-falling (捺), dot (点), rising (提), turning (折), and hook (钩).

Components are made up of strokes. Some components can stand alone as independent characters, while others cannot and must combine with other components to form a complete character.

Complete characters consist of one or more components. Structurally, there are two main types of characters:

Single-component characters (独体字)—formed by a single component.

Compound characters (合体字)—formed by two or more components.

The writing of Chinese characters follows specific stroke order rules, known as “笔顺 (bǐshùn)”.

## 二、汉字 Chinese Characters

#### 汉字书写（六） Chinese Character Writing (VI)

1 认识汉字结构：独体字与合体字 Introduction to Chinese Character Structures: Single-Component Characters and Compound Characters

独体字和合体字是汉字的两种基本结构。独体字是由基本笔画构成的不能拆分为两个或两个以上部件或偏旁的汉字，例如：“人”就是由一撇（丿）一捺（丶）两个笔画构成的独体字。合体字是由两个或两个以上的部件或偏旁组成的汉字，例如：“认”就是由言字旁“讠”和“人”组成的合体字。

形声字，即由形旁和声旁组成的合体字。形旁主要表示意义类别，声旁主要提示字音。多数形声字可以通过形旁快速确定字义类别，借助声旁辅助推测读音，能有效帮助我们理解和记忆汉字。例如：“奶、姥、妈、姐、妹”，左边的“女”为形旁，表示这些字的意义与女性相关，右边的“乃、老、马、且、未”是声旁，可以通过声旁推测这些字的读音。

Chinese characters fall into two basic structural types: single-component characters and compound characters. A single-component character is a Chinese character formed by basic strokes and cannot be divided into two or more components or radicals. For example: “人(rén)” is a single-component character composed of two strokes—one left-falling stroke (丿) and one right-falling stroke (丶). A compound character consists of two or more components, which can be single-component characters or radicals. For instance: “认(rèn)” is a compound character formed by the semantic radical “讠” and the “人”.

A phono-semantic compound character is a compound Chinese character composed of a semantic radical and a phonetic radical. The semantic radical primarily indicates the category of meaning, while the phonetic radical mainly provides a cue for pronunciation. For most phono-semantic compound characters, the semantic radical enables us to quickly identify the general meaning category, and the phonetic radical helps infer the pronunciation, which can effectively assist us in understanding and memorizing Chinese characters. For example: in the characters “奶”, “姥”, “妈”, “姐”, and “妹”, the component “ㄆ” on the left functions as the semantic radical, denoting meanings related to females, while the components “乃”, “老”, “马”, “且”, and “未” on the right serve as phonetic radicals, through which the pronunciations of these characters can be inferred.

### 2 听辨句调 Sentence Intonation Identification

按照不同表述功能和语气划分，汉语句子一般分为陈述句、疑问句、感叹句和祈使句四类。

陈述句叙述事实，句尾音调平或略下降；疑问句提出问题，除了特指问句，句尾音调多上扬；感叹句表达强烈的情感，句尾音调拉长或加重；祈使句发出请求或命令，句尾音调下降。

According to different expressive functions and intonations, Chinese sentences are generally classified into declarative, interrogative, exclamatory, and imperative sentences.

A declarative sentence states a fact; its intonation is level or slightly falling at the end. An interrogative sentence asks a question. Except for specific questions (wh-questions), the intonation typically rises at the end. An exclamatory sentence conveys strong emotions, with

the ending intonation often lengthened or stressed. An imperative sentence makes a request or issues a command, typically ending with a falling intonation.

### 听录音，辨别听到的是陈述句还是疑问句。陈述句用A标出，疑问句用B标出，并大声朗读。

Listen and identify whether each sentence you hear is declarative or interrogative. Mark declarative sentences with A and interrogative sentences with B, then read aloud.

(1) Nǐ shì xuéshēng ma (B)

(2) Nǐ shì nǎ guó rén ()

(3) Wǒ jiào Lǐ Wén ( )

(4) Jīntiān wǒ xiūxi ( )

(5) Shéi shì lǎoshī ( )

(6) Wǒ xiǎng qù chāoshì mǎi dōngxi ( )

听录音，辨别听到的句子类型。陈述句用A标出，疑问句用B标出，感叹句用C标出，祈使句用D标出，并大声朗读。

## 模拟测试

### HSK（一级）模拟试卷

### HSK Mock Test (Level 1)

### 注意

一、HSK（一级）分两部分，共40题：

1. 听力（20题，约12分钟）

2. 阅读（20题，20分钟）

二、听力结束后，有3分钟填写答题卡。

三、全部考试约40分钟（含考生填写个人信息时间5分钟）。

# 第四部分

第 36—40 题

Qǐngwèn xiànzài jǐ diǎn le?

例如：请问现在几点了？

Shuōhuàrén xiǎng zhīdào shénme?

★说话人想知道什么？

shíjiān míngzi fángjiānhào

A 时间 ✓ B 名字 C 房间号

Xià yǔ le, wǒmen zuò chۆzüchē huí jiā ba.

36. 下雨了，我们坐出租车回家吧。

 $ \underset{\cdot}{S} $huōhuàrén xiǎng zěnme huí jiā?

★说话人 想 怎么 回家?

kǎichē zuò fēijī zuò chūzǔchē

A 开车 B 坐飞机 C 坐出租车

Wǒ érzi jīnnián suì le, zhèngzài shàng zhōngxué.

37. 我儿子今年16岁了，正在上中学。

Shuōhuàrén de érzi shì:

★说话人的儿子是：

xiǎoxuéshēng zhōngxuéshēng dàxuéshēng

A 小学生 B 中学生 C 大学生

Xiǎo māo hé xiǎo gǒu dōu zài fángjiān wài shuìjiào ne.

). 小 猫 和 小 狗 都在 房 间 外 睡 觉 呢。

38. 小猫和小狗都在房间外睡觉呢。

Xiǎo gǒu zài:

★小狗在：

A 桌子 下 B 房间里 C 房间 外

zhuōzi xià fángjiān li fángjiān wài

Wõ tóngxué zài zhège xuéxiào gōngzuò hěn duō nián le, nǐ yǒu wèntí kěyǐ wèn tā.

39. 我同学在这个学校工作很多年了，你有问题可以问他。

Shuōhuàren de tóngxué zài nǎr shàngbān?

★说话人的同学在哪儿上班？

fàndiàn yīyuàn xuéxiào

A 饭店 B 医院 C 学校

Zhège xiâopéngyǒu xiě zì zhēn hǎokàn.

★那个 小朋友：

míngzi hǎotīng xiě zì hǎokàn zhèngzài dúshū

A 名字 好听 B 写字 好看 C 正在 读书
